

# MODULE enterprise  (verifier confidence: high)
nomenclature: The Enterprise pages barely use lib/industry-config.ts TERMS. SearchResults group titles (Persons, Organizations, Tickets, Invoices...) are hard-coded (SearchResults.tsx:129-217), as are the Dashboard stat tiles (Dashboard.tsx:407-430). Under TERMS these would read: utilities Contacts/Organizations/Invoices/Tickets (industry-config.ts:94-100); financial tickets='Service Requests', leads='Prospects', opportunities='Deals' (industry-config.ts:262-275); government_federal = GOVERNMENT_TERMS + products 'Government Services': Constituents/Businesses/Statements/Service Requests/Applicants/Grants-Projects (industry-config.ts:50-62, 136). Industry-driven text that does exist: AnalyticsDashboard subtitle `${terminology.industryLabel} overview` ('Utility', 'Financial Services', 'Government: Federal') (AnalyticsDashboard.tsx:51; useSettings.ts:41-52). Dashboard outage card title terminology.outageMap ('Outage Map' for utilities/financial, 'Disaster/Weather Map' for government_federal), shown only for utilities/telecom (useSettings.ts:43-52; Dashboard.tsx:333, 1457-1462). Analytics sections vary by industry: utilities/telecom add Usage; financial with bankingDemo adds Network Pulse (analytics-dashboard-config.ts:26-35). The nav renames persons/organizations per industry (useNavigation.ts L49-52), which does not reach these pages.

recipeChanges: Recon rule 1: home Dashboard stops being 'for nobody in particular' and becomes the personal Enterprise home. Widgets redistribute: contact-center pulse, hourly calls, live agents, softphone/phone dock -> Support > Agent desk; Data Health and MRR -> Finance; outage map -> Operations; 'my tickets' and the inbox -> Enterprise > Inbox. Recipe enterpriseHome (modules.ts:323-345) shows stats Inbox unread / My open tickets / Due this week / Workflows running and panels My inbox, Workflow runs, Analytics highlights. JAREIS Radar tab is not named in the recon; nearest homes are Enterprise > Analytics or AI agents (open question).

Should merge the bell popover (notifications: QA types ticket.sla_breached, ai_usage.digest, exchange.awaiting_review/failed, appointment.booked/cancelled, partner.invitation_received/revoked, automation.ownerless; categories job/support/billing/network/scheduling/automation), my assigned tickets (MY_TICKETS_HREF), and approvals/access requests into one personal list. Personas: utilities 5 active + 4 draft, financial 6 active + 2 draft, government_federal 4 active.

## dashboard widgets
- My Tickets <stat> source=GET /api/tickets/stats?mine=true (useTickets.ts:97-104); open set new, triaged, assigned, in_progress, waiting_customer, waiting_internal, reopened metric=open tickets assigned to me + in progress + waiting (customer+internal)
- Recent Tickets <list> source=GET /api/dashboard/summary .recentTickets metric=latest tickets: #number, subject, status badge, priority badge
- Notifications / inbox <feed> source=/api/notifications?limit=30, unread count, pending access requests metric=unread notifications (title, body, relative time) + pending access requests
- Stat tiles: Contacts / Organizations / Open Tickets / Interactions Mine/Other <stat> source=GET /api/dashboard/summary metric=totalContacts, totalOrganizations, openTickets, interactionsToday mine/others
- Sync status banner <feed> source=useSyncQueueJobs limit 10, refetch 10s metric=active/queued sync jobs, last completed (<1h) or failed run
- Setup / Demo checklist + UsageNudge <list> source=SetupChecklist (landNowEnabled flag), DemoChecklist (demo_active), UsageNudge metric=onboarding steps remaining; demo 'Start here' tasks; plan usage nudge
- Workflow runs <table> source=workflow_executions (GET /api/workflows/:id/executions today; no cross-workflow feed exists) metric=recent executions: workflow, status, trigger, started
- Analytics highlights <stat> source=/api/analytics/query/revenue-summary, /api/analytics/query/mrr-trend metric=revenue this month (RevenueCard), MRR trend sparkline
- Command Console <chat> source=useAgentCommand -> agent_command_runs metric=natural-language command + channel; output log with summary metrics and 'View results' link
- Moves OUT of Enterprise home (for reference) <chart(bar)/map/softphone> source=useAgentDashboard, useDataHealth (30s), useOutageMap, CcpPanel metric=Contact Center pulse (Total, Answered, Missed, Wait, Handle, SLA%, Abandon%), Hourly Call Volume bars, Live Agent Status, Live Caller Context, Phone dock (Connect CCP) -> Support > Agent desk; Data Health (Accounts, Contacts, Organizations, AR Invoices, AR Linked %, Payments, With Balance %) and MRR -> Finance; Outage Map (terminology.outageMap) -> Operations

- Workflow builder canvas (React Flow / @xyflow) with drag-from-palette nodes and edges @ WorkflowBuilder.tsx:12-18, 296-330; pages/workflows/nodes/*, edges/*, StepPalette: flow builder with drag-and-drop canvas; not composable from ui-atoms parts
- JAREIS Radar hero + drilldown previews and audience side panel @ JareisRadarDashboard.tsx:420-470, 830-920: mostly cards/lists (composable), but payment-conversion breakdown bars and the gradient hero are custom; drilldown preview is a data table in a sheet
- Bulk message composer with HTML template preview and merge-tag editor @ CommandResults.tsx:440-485 (BulkSendMessageDialog, BulkMergeTagsEditor, previewHtml): HTML email preview / rich content rendering
- Marketing Hub creative studio (image/video variants), visitor tracking, funnel bars @ MarketingAnalytics.tsx:717-721, 1006-1130, 1976-2015 (route hidden): media editor and funnel chart; route is commented out, low priority

## open questions
- Where does JAREIS Radar (/dashboard/jareis, gated on aiAgents surface radar_dashboard) land: an Analytics section, an AI agents tab, or part of the Enterprise home? The recon does not place it.
- Does the Dashboard Command Console (natural-language command -> agent_command_runs -> /command-results/:id) belong to Search or to AI agents? It runs GLOBAL_SEARCH and is reachable only from the Dashboard.
- Enterprise > Inbox vs Support > Inbox: the recon calls Enterprise Inbox 'all my work' and Support Inbox 'threads + escalations'. Confirm Enterprise Inbox is notifications + my assigned tickets + approvals/access requests, and not customer threads.
- Workflows status filter: nav tabs (All/Active/Drafts) and in-page buttons (All/Active/Draft/Paused/Archived) disagree. Recon section 5 assumes chips; adopt the 5-value set?
- Should the platform-admin /manage/workflows (cross-tenant, tenant selector, Success Rate column) stay out of the tenant Enterprise module?
- Do Notification Settings (/settings/notifications) move under Enterprise > Inbox or stay in the account menu with the rest of Admin?
- Home stat tiles (Contacts/Organizations/Open Tickets/Interactions) are not assigned by recon rule 1; drop them from the personal home or keep them as cross-module counts?
- There is no ⌘K command palette in apps/web today (only components/ui/command.tsx used in pickers), although the intent docs expect one (03-intent.md:89). Should Enterprise > Search add it?

## QA profile
notes: All profiled tables are tenant-scoped (tenant_id uuid present everywhere). Tables named in the map that do NOT exist: 'access requests' (no table; users has no pending column - pending domain-access requests plausibly = users.is_active=false: utilities 4, financial 0, government_federal 0; unverified), 'sync job queue' (BullMQ, not in Postgres; nearest tables erp_sync_log, file_sync_jobs, file_sync_runs not profiled), 'usage records' exists as usage_records, 'network pulse synthetic series' = banking_network_pulse (only tenant green-bank, none of the personas), jareis_actions and jareis_insights exist but are EMPTY in whole DB, ai_budgets EMPTY, agent_command_result_rows EMPTY, workflow_send_logs EMPTY. 'persons' table does not exist (contacts is the person table). Correction vs. computed task: ar for utilities has only 128 rows in QA (government_federal 35,626, financial 15,747; 155,207 total). Contacts: utilities 7,105 rows but 6,998 soft-deleted (is_deleted=true) -> only 107 live; financial 1,255 live; government_federal 2,547 live. Organizations live: utilities 99, financial 1,164, government_federal 117. Home stat-tile plausible values: utilities contacts 107 / orgs 99 / open tickets 8; financial 1,255 / 1,164 / ~342 non-closed; government_federal 2,547 / 117 / ~868 open-ish. Tickets: government_federal 1,213 tickets all unassigned (assigned_to null), statuses evenly distributed (synthetic seed); financial 376 of 489 assigned (so 'My tickets' only meaningful for financial); utilities 8 tickets all new/medium. Ticket numbers integer, gov 931-4839, financial 174-795; subject avg 41 chars, max 60. Notifications: financial 1,687 (unread 1,317), utilities 126 (unread 113), gov 95 (unread 59); whole-table categories include job=1,382 but no persona has job notifications; title avg 30 chars, body avg 66 chars (~10% null). Interactions: only financial (851) and utilities (550) have meaningful data; gov 1; date ranges are past (no 'today' rows), so Interactions Mine/Other today = 0/0 in QA. Workflows match computed task (232 workflows; persona utilities 5 active+4 draft, financial 6+2, gov 4 active); all triggers manual, event_type null; 21 executions only in utilities (3 completed/10 failed), financial (5 failed) and 2 other tenants; median execution duration ~0.05s; executions 2026-06-23..2026-10-26. Agent command runs: 3 total (utilities: GLOBAL_SEARCH completed 1, UNKNOWN unsupported 2), 2026-07-23..07-27. AI usage: provider openai only; models gpt-4o-mini, gpt-5.6-sol, sora-2, gpt-image-1; per-persona spend cents: utilities jareis_chat 776.21 + agent_command 0.93; financial jareis_chat 323.09 + media_generation 1010.00; gov jareis_chat 337.73 + media_generation 1200.00. Orgs/workspaces/teams: utilities 2/3/2, financial 2/2/2, gov 8/1/1 (one tenant-per-org pattern across 673 tenants). Reference formats: invoices INV-999999 (most), INV-YYYYMMDD-HHMMSS-NNNNNN (test), AMEX-INV-9999-99999, GOV-IRS-9999, GOV-BFS-9999, CONED-STMT-999999; payments PAY-AR-999999, PAY-AP-999999, PAY-GOV-IRS-9999; vouchers SUPP-BILL-9999-99999, AMEX-BILL-9999-99999, INBOX-BILL-9999-9999, EXP-9999-99999; quotes Q-<13 digits> (epoch ms); subscriptions external_id SUB-9999-9999; outages BCN-XXXXXXXX-XX. Currency USD everywhere. Names: contacts 'First Last' person names; organizations company names; organizations.industry is dirty free text (Utilities/utilities, test values 'Indosf-<epoch>-<hex>', 'Export Testing'). jareis_conversations.page_context contains E2E test labels (not reproduced). Free-text error messages, subjects, bodies intentionally omitted.

# MODULE network  (verifier confidence: high)
nomenclature: lib/industry-config.ts renames the Contacts/Organizations labels (page title, empty states, 'Add X' button, footer count) through useIndustryLabel, and useNavigation.ts:50-76 renames the sidebar item and its facet tabs (nav key 'persons' maps to 'contacts'; 'All Partners' becomes 'All <name>'). utilities (industry-config.ts:94-133): Contacts / Organizations, Add Contact / Add Organization; person facets Customers/Vendors/Employees; org facets Commercial/Vendors/Internal. financial (262-301): Contacts / Organizations, Add Contact / Add Organization; person facets Retail Clients/Vendors/Employees; org facets Corporate Clients/Vendors/Internal; Organizations also gets the Card products filter section (Organizations.tsx:146-149, partner-filter-defs.ts:84-96). government_federal (136, inherits GOVERNMENT_TERMS 51-90): Constituents / Businesses, Register Constituent / Register Business; person facets Residents/Contractors/Staff; org facets Commercial/Suppliers/Agencies. Network pages (Partners, Directory, Exchanges) and DuplicateReview's title are not translated, though DuplicateReview's entity badges use getLabel('contact'/'organization'). The contacts page eyebrow is hard-coded 'Partners' (Contacts.tsx:594, Organizations.tsx:581).

## Contacts
pages: /contacts -> apps/web/src/pages/Contacts.tsx (App.tsx:257; nav PARTNERS > Persons, navigation.ts:225-237); /contacts?facet=customers|vendors|employees (sidebar tabs, navigation.ts:232-235); /duplicates -> pages/DuplicateReview.tsx (contact half; nav ADMIN > Record Consolidation, navigation.ts:417-423) - shared with Organizations
tables: contacts, accounts (linked account, balance, past due), parties / party sources (source badges), subscriptions (filters), outages (filters)
columns: (select) <checkbox> (row selection state (local)) | Name <link (/contacts/:id) + badges> (contacts.first_name + last_name; + sourceCount 'Merged from N sources' badge when >1; + PrimarySourceBadge(contact.sources)) | Email <text, '—' when null> (contacts.email) | Phone <text> (contacts.phone, fallback contacts.mobile) | Account # <link (/accounts/:id), 'View account' when number missing, '—' when unlinked> (linked account (contact.accountId / accountNumber)) | Premise Address <text, truncated 200px with title tooltip> (contact.premiseAddress, fallback contact.address (formatMaybeJsonAddress)) | Balance <money (Intl USD currency formatter, Contacts.tsx:109-118)> (contact.balance (account balance)) | Past Due Date <date> (contact.pastDueDate) | Created <date> (contacts.created_at) | Actions <kebab dropdown> (row menu)
filters: facet-tab/Sidebar tabs ?facet=: All <Contacts>, customers (Customers / per-industry), vendors, employees ; search/Search (URL ?q=): name, email, phone, balance > 500, past due > 10/12/25 (operator syntax) ; text/Location: ZIP code / City / State-province: zip, city, state ; select/Outage status (multi): reported, confirmed, crew_assigned, crew_en_route, crew_on_site, restored, closed ; search/Outage number: outageNumber text ; select/Subscription status (multi): draft, trialing, active, paused, past_due, canceled, expired ; select/Subscription product (multi, dynamic from products) + per-tenant subscription custom select fields: dynamic ; select/Balance (numericOp): gt, gte, lt, lte, eq, between ; date-range/Due date (dueWindow): overdue by N days, within N days, source: AR due date | subscription next billing ; toggle/Past due (tri-state): any, yes, no ; toggle/Has linked account (tri-state): any, yes, no ; select/Account type: customer, vendor, employee, household, internal ; search/Account status (text): e.g. active ; date-range/Created after / before: createdFrom, createdTo ; search/Email contains / Phone contains: email, phone ; toggle/Has email / Has phone (tri-state): hasEmail, hasPhone ; select/Meta (custom field) filters, discovered on expand: string/number/date ops: eq, gt, gte, lt, lte, has
rowActions: Edit (link /contacts/:id?action=edit), Link or merge (only when ERP enabled+connected and contacts.update), Delete (contacts.delete, confirm dialog), Row click on Name -> detail
pageActions: Import (file import wizard), Export (CSV import/export modal), Add Contact (create dialog: first/last name, email, phone, mobile, title, department, address, work role/type, is-customer/customer type, organization, ERP sync providers), Bulk (on selection): Deselect All (n), Refresh from ERP (n), Create Audience (n), Enroll in Subscription (n), Send Application (n), Send Campaign (n, only when >1), Send Message (n)
countBadges: Header chip: active org name (green) or 'All Organizations' (purple) when multi-org (Contacts.tsx:597-606), Footer 'Showing N of TOTAL <contacts>' (total from separate useContactsTotal query, spinner until loaded), No sidebar count badge
sorting: URL-backed (useUrlSort) on Name(firstName), Email, Created; default createdAt desc; SortField also allows lastName (Contacts.tsx:107,148,740-787)
pagination: Offset Previous/Next with 'Page X of Y' (URL-backed useUrlPagination; total fetched lazily, hasNextPage inferred until total known) (Contacts.tsx:893-920)
empty: Search active: 'No <contacts> found' / 'Try adjusting your search criteria.'; otherwise 'No <contacts> yet' / 'Get started by adding your first <contact> or importing from a CSV file.' with [Add Contact] and [Import CSV] buttons (Contacts.tsx:702-725). Error: 'Failed to load <contacts>. Please try again.'
recipeChanges: Moves from PARTNERS > Persons into Network > Contacts. Recon open point (section 5) assumes the ?facet= sidebar tabs (All/Customers/Vendors/Employees) become filter chips on the list. Duplicate review (Record Consolidation, today under ADMIN) has no menu slot in the Network module; nearest home is a 'Possible duplicates' entry/chip inside Contacts and Organizations (not stated by recon - see open questions). Label stays industry-translated.

## Organizations
pages: /organizations -> pages/Organizations.tsx (App.tsx:259; nav PARTNERS > Organizations, navigation.ts:238-250); /organizations?facet=customers|vendors|employees (navigation.ts:245-248); /network/partners -> pages/NetworkPartners.tsx (App.tsx:409; nav NETWORK > Partners, navigation.ts:350-355) - deleted per recon rule 2, folded in; /network/partners/:id -> pages/NetworkPartnerDetail.tsx (App.tsx:410) - becomes Network tab on organization detail (agreements, exchange history); /duplicates -> pages/DuplicateReview.tsx (organization half)

filters: facet-tab/Sidebar tabs ?facet=: All <Organizations>, customers (Commercial / per-industry), vendors, employees (Internal / per-industry) ; search/Search (?q=): name, account #, phone, balance > 500, past due > 10/12/25 ; text/Shared fields: ZIP, City, State, Outage status, Outage number, Subscription status, Balance op, Due window, Past due, Has linked account, Account type, Account status, Created after/before: same as Contacts ; search/Email contains / Phone contains / Industry: email, phone, industry ; toggle/Has phone / Has industry (tri-state): hasPhone, hasIndustry ; select/Card products (financial industry only): Card program: platinum, gold, green, Virtual card downloaded (tri-state), Card limit min, Card limit max ; select/Subscription product + subscription custom fields + meta filters: dynamic ; select/NetworkPartners: Network status: all, not_invited, invited, active (Connected), suspended, revoked ; select/NetworkPartners: Type: all, partner, supplier, customer
rowActions: Edit, Link or merge (ERP connected), Delete (organizations.delete), From NetworkPartners: Invite (not invited) / Re-invite (invited) / Manage (active|suspended|revoked -> partner detail)
pageActions: Import, Export, Add Organization (create dialog), Bulk on selection: Deselect All, Refresh from ERP (when ERP enabled), Send Application, Enroll in Subscription, Send Campaign (>1), Send Message, From NetworkPartners: Bring your book, Invite by email, Invite all (confirm: 'send N invitations', counts.invitable), From NetworkPartners page panels: IncomingConnectRequests, HeldAcceptancesPanel, Directory publication consent panel, From NetworkPartnerDetail: Add Agreement, auto-accept switch, revoke agreement, Generate Invitation, Suspend, Reactivate, Revoke, View Exchanges
countBadges: Header org chip as Contacts, Footer 'Showing N of TOTAL <organizations> (k filters applied)', NetworkPartners summary line: 'N organizations · N with email · N with phone' (NetworkPartners.tsx:225-235)
sorting: Name, Created clickable (default createdAt desc); SortField also 'industry' with no header (Organizations.tsx:104,144,722-760). NetworkPartners: no sorting
pagination: Organizations: Previous/Next + 'Page X of Y' (URL page). NetworkPartners: PAGE_SIZE 25, Previous/Next shown only when >1 page
empty: Search active: 'No <organizations> found' / 'Try adjusting your search criteria.'; else 'No <organizations> yet' / 'Get started by adding your first <organization>.' with [Add Organization] [Import CSV] (Organizations.tsx:684-707). NetworkPartners: 'No organizations match your filters.' (NetworkPartners.tsx:284)

Organizations list gains the Network status column (Connected/Invited/Suspended/Revoked/Not invited) plus Invite / Re-invite / Connect actions (and Invite by email / Invite all / Bring your book page actions); organization detail gains a Network tab with agreements and exchange history (replacing NetworkPartnerDetail). Facet sidebar tabs become chips (section 5). Network status can be a chip/select filter.

recipeChanges: Becomes 'Activity' = exchange accept/reject feed (module table). Rule 3: an exchange is a transport envelope, not an inbox; document exchanges also surface as a delivery badge + timeline on invoice/bill/order/quote detail. Inbox/Outbox tabs collapse to direction chips. Statements (/network/statements) leaves Network for a 'Partner settlements' filter on Finance Receivables/Payables. Partner ID column should resolve to the organization name (recipe concern). Invites sub-view likely merges with Organizations network status/invites.

## dashboard widgets
- Organizations <stat> source=Dashboard.tsx stats summary.totalOrganizations via useDashboardSummary; Data Health dataHealth.organizations metric=count of organizations (+ new this week in recipe)
- Contacts <stat> source=Dashboard.tsx summary.totalContacts; Data Health dataHealth.contacts metric=count of contacts
- Connected on Enterprise <stat> source=/api/network/roster networkStatus (no dashboard widget today) metric=organizations with network status active / % of organizations
- Pending invites <stat> source=network_partner_invitations status pending / expiresAt (roster) metric=invitations pending (+ expiring within 7 days)
- Exchanges awaiting review <stat> source=/api/network/stats byStatus.transport_delivered (today the Exchanges nav badge) metric=inbound exchanges in transport_delivered
- Exchange stats (Total / Inbound / Outbound / Failed 24h) <stat> source=/api/network/stats metric=totalExchanges, inbound, outbound, failedLast24h
- Connect requests <list> source=IncomingConnectRequests component on NetworkPartners metric=incoming partner connect requests
- Recent exchanges <feed> source=useNetworkExchanges metric=latest network_exchanges with direction/status
- Directory matches <list> source=new (network_companies x organizations); no source today metric=organizations in book present in directory but not connected
- Network Pulse (financial demo) <chart(area+bar)> source=analytics queries network_pulse_trend / network_pulse_latest (AnalyticsDashboard networkPulse section) metric=synthetic indices (regional stress, consumer spend, deposit flow, credit demand/supply, SMB health, fraud trend): daily trend, latest by region, 7-day change

- Wide 10-column table with horizontal scroll (min-w 800px), select-all, and sticky bulk action bar @ Contacts.tsx:729-891, Organizations.tsx:712-865: table part has no built-in selection or bulk toolbar; at the QA scale (thousands of rows) and the spec's 5M+ contacts, column resizing or virtualization may be expected
- File import wizard with CSV column mapping @ LazyFileImportWizard / CsvImportExportModal on Contacts and Organizations: Needs a file drop zone and a column-mapper grid; no file-drop part
- Network Pulse area/bar charts @ components/analytics/dashboard-sections.tsx:207-252: Charts (Recharts) are not in the part list
- Duplicate merge review (pick survivor record, field-by-field compare, make primary) @ DuplicateReview.tsx, MergeReviewDialog, MergedGroupCard, ConsolidationRulesPanel: A side-by-side compare and merge UI with infinite 'Load more' card lists; can be built from dialog+radio-group+card, but the field diff view is bespoke
- Directory company card grid with logo @ NetworkDirectory.tsx:39-121,267-278: Simple: card+avatar+button composes fine (listed for completeness, low risk)

## open questions
- Where does Record Consolidation (/duplicates, today ADMIN > Record Consolidation) go? It spans contacts and organizations and the Network menu has no slot. Options: a 'Possible duplicates' chip/side panel on each list, or Admin/account menu.
- Directory publication consent panel, Bring your book, Incoming connect requests and Held acceptances live on NetworkPartners today. Does each move to the Organizations list header, to the Network dashboard ('Connect requests' panel) or to settings?
- The Invites sub-view of Exchanges (external email offers with a payment status) fits neither the Activity feed nor Organizations cleanly. Fold it into the Organizations network status, or keep it as an Activity chip?
- Activity in QA: no exchange is in transport_delivered, so the accept/reject feed and the 'awaiting review' stat are 0 for every persona. The mock needs synthetic pending rows.
- The Partner ID column shows a truncated uuid today. Should Activity resolve it to the organization name (the join through network_partners.linked_organization_id)?
- The 'Proposals awaiting your review' section (applications.review) sits on Exchanges. Does it stay in Network > Activity or move to Engage > Applications?
- Network Statements leaves Network per rule 3. Confirm nothing about statements stays on the Network dashboard.
- Network Pulse (financial demo analytics) is not assigned by the recon. Is it a Network dashboard widget or Enterprise > Analytics?

'subscriptions' in the map is customer_subscriptions (public.subscriptions is the tenant billing-plan table). Soft-deletes matter a lot: utilities (beacon) has 7105 contact rows but only 107 live, and 3108 organizations but only 99 live. The map's persona counts are raw rows, so the utilities Contacts/Organizations lists are nearly empty in QA. Contacts enums were profiled on government_federal and Organizations on financial (most live rows). Balance, Past Due Date and Account # come from accounts.attributes (balance, dueDate, pastDueAmount strings) and accounts.external_account_id. Only financial and gov carry balances; utilities has 4 accounts with balance 10. Network data is thin: no suspended partners, no exchanges in transport_delivered or failed, 0 connect requests, 0 pre-exchanges/invites, 0 agreement proposals, and 3 directory companies with no industry, region or logo. Connected organizations are under 1% in every persona (1/99, 4/1164, 0/117). The mock needs synthetic rows for awaiting-review exchanges, suspended partners, invites and directory cards. Pending invitations are mostly past expires_at but still have status pending. QA dates run 2026-03..2026-11 against today 2026-11-10.

# MODULE engage  (verifier confidence: high)
nomenclature: Only the Applications pages call useIndustryLabel inside Engage. They use four keys: applicationForms, submissions, applicationForm and submission. All three persona industries resolve them to the same strings: "Application forms", "Submissions", "Application form" and "Submission". The definitions are at utilities apps/web/src/lib/industry-config.ts:104-113, financial :272-281, and government_federal (GOVERNMENT_TERMS spread) :61-70 and :136. The keys are used at Applications.tsx:319 (stat card "Application forms"), :327 (stat card "Submissions"), ApplicationSubmissions.tsx:75 (page title) and :158 (column header "Application form"). In practice, no Engage label changes per industry today. GOVERNMENT_TERMS does rename leads to "Applicants" (industry-config.ts:58), but no Engage page reads that key. OutreachCampaigns, CampaignAudiences, NudgeDashboard, NudgeMailbox and SocialHub/CC* do not call getLabel. The navigation group (navigation.ts:131-190) has no industry overrides.

## Campaigns
pages: /campaigns -> apps/web/src/pages/OutreachCampaigns.tsx (nav item 'Campaigns', navigation.ts:171-176, App.tsx:383); /campaigns/:id -> apps/web/src/pages/OutreachCampaignDetail.tsx (App.tsx:385; tabs Definition / Runs via ?tab=); /campaigns/:id/wizard -> apps/web/src/pages/CampaignWizard.tsx (App.tsx:386; steps Audience / Message / Cap / Schedule / Review); /marketing/campaigns, /marketing/* (commented out, 'Marketing hidden for now', App.tsx:357-377, navigation.ts:137-151): not live
tables: outreach_campaigns, outreach_campaign_groups, outreach_campaign_runs, outreach_messages (campaign recipients, source='campaign'), outreach_audience_files / outreach_audience_rows (CSV audience), audience_plans (audience step)
columns: Campaign <text (name + mono id subline)> (outreach_campaigns.name (bold) + outreach_campaigns.id (mono, second line)) | Status <badge(status): draft=Draft, scheduled=Scheduled, running=Running, paused=Paused, completed=Completed, icon + colour (CampaignStatusBadge.tsx:6-12)> (outreach_campaigns.status) | Channel <badge(channel): Email / SMS / WhatsApp / IVR with icon (ChannelBadge.tsx:8-22)> (outreach_campaigns.channel) | Group <badge (secondary), else muted 'No group'> (outreach_campaigns.nudge_group_id -> outreach_campaign_groups.name) | Last run <relative date, else muted 'Never run'> (outreach_campaigns.last_run_at) | Next run <datetime, else '—'> (outreach_campaigns.next_run_at (only when status='scheduled'), schedule_kind='once_now' -> 'On Finish') | Modified <date> (outreach_campaigns.updated_at) | (actions, no header) <kebab dropdown> (row menu)
filters: facet-tab/Campaign channel (?channel=, default email; pill tabs with count): Email, SMS, WhatsApp, IVR
rowActions: Edit (opens /campaigns/:id/wizard), Runs (opens /campaigns/:id?tab=runs), Delete (ConfirmDialog 'Delete Campaign'), Row double-click opens the wizard
pageActions: Audiences (outline button -> /campaigns/audiences), New Campaign (modal: Campaign name with live name-availability check, Channel select, optional Campaign group select with 'Create a group' sub-modal: group name + frequency cap: No more than N sends / Window days / Min days between / Exclude weekends / Exclude account holidays), Detail page header: Pause (disabled), Duplicate (disabled), Edit, open wizard, Run now (confirm 'Run this campaign now?'), No import, export or bulk actions
countBadges: Count pill on each channel tab (count of campaigns per channel, OutreachCampaigns.tsx:320-331), No nav badge
sorting: None interactive. Rows render in API order; the DataTable has a sorting model, but the headers are plain strings with no toggles.
pagination: Client-side DataTable, 10 rows per page, 'Page X of Y' with Previous/Next. The Runs and Recipients sub-tables are server-paged with 'a–b of N' and Previous/Next.
empty: Title 'No campaigns yet', description 'Create your first campaign to reach an audience over Email, SMS, IVR or WhatsApp.', action 'New Campaign' (OutreachCampaigns.tsx:307-313). The DataTable falls back to 'No results.' when the active channel tab is empty.
recipeChanges: The recon (section 4, Engage row and rule 4) keeps Campaigns as an Engage menu item and names outreach_campaigns as Engage-owned (Campaigns shared object). The section 5 open point assumes the recipe turns sidebar/tab filters into chips, so the channel tabs become channel filter chips with counts. The 'Audiences' header button goes away because Audiences is now a sibling menu item. Campaign detail (Definition / Runs) and the wizard stay as drill-ins under Campaigns. No new columns are proposed; the deferred 'Audience' column comes from the mockup comment at OutreachCampaigns.tsx:197-201.

rowActions: Submit (draft / needs_attention only), Edit (opens 'Edit audience' modal with PlanBuilder; not shown when retired), Retire (ConfirmDialog; not shown when retired), Approvals tab card: Approve (carries plan hash; may need a second approver), Reject (modal with reason, min 3 chars), Segments page (AudienceSegmentsSettings): click row to edit, Submit (draft), Reject / Approve (pending_approval)
pageActions: New audience (modal: Name, Description, PlanBuilder condition groups + recipient rule, live count), Segments page: New segment (modal: Name, Description, raw SQL Predicate textarea, Test predicate button with count badge), No import, export or bulk actions

empty: Audiences tab: title 'No saved audiences yet', description 'Build an audience once from approved fields, get it approved, and reuse it in any campaign.', action 'New audience'. With filters: 'No audiences match.' Approvals tab: title 'Nothing to approve', description 'When someone submits an audience that your policy says needs review, it waits here.' Segments page: 'No segments yet' / 'Create one when a campaign's audience needs something the query builder can't express.' with 'New segment'.
recipeChanges: Becomes the Engage 'Audiences' menu item, renamed from 'Audiences & approvals' (recon section 4 Engage row; audience_plans is an Engage-owned Campaigns object, section 2 shared-objects table line 56). The recon text proposes neither. The orphan Saved segments page (/settings/audience-segments, no nav entry) is a natural candidate to fold in as a second source type, but the recon does not mention it.

pagination: None. The API returns {data,total,limit,offset}, but the UI renders a single unpaged list of cards.

(?q=, 500ms debounce, server): free text ; status-tab/Forms status (?status=, button group): All, Draft, Published, Archived ; search/Submissions: 'Search by name, email, phone...' (?q=, 300ms debounce, server): free text ; select/Submissions: form filter (?formId=): All application forms, <each form name> ; status-tab/Submissions status (local state, not in URL): All, Pending, Under Review, Approved, Rejected, Converted

sorting: None interactive. Server order.
pagination: Forms: URL-backed Pagination, page sizes 20 / 50 / 100 (default 50), 'Showing n of N application forms'. Submissions: no pager; it shows the first API page (default limit 50) with a 'Showing n of N submissions' footer.
empty: Forms: 'No application forms yet' / 'Create your first application form to start collecting submissions.' + 'New application form'; when filtered: 'No application forms found' / 'Try adjusting your search or filter.'. Submissions: 'No submissions yet' / 'Submissions will appear here when customers fill out your published forms.'; when filtered: 'No submissions found' / 'Try adjusting your search or filter.'

The recon notes that submission detail deep-links into Network (/contacts, /organizations), Sales (/leads, /opportunities) and Finance (/invoices) (recon lines 61-64), so cross-module links on submission detail should stay. ## dashboard widgets
- Engage dashboard (module root): no home-Dashboard widget is redistributed here <note> source=Recon section 4 rule 1 (docs/v2/recon/2026-11-10-authenticated-ui-duplication-recon.md:82-84) metric=
- Campaigns live <stat> source=outreach_campaigns.status / next_run_at. Today only per-channel counts are shown (OutreachCampaigns.tsx:72-73,320-331) metric=count of campaigns by status (running / scheduled), recipe stat 'Campaigns live'
- Sent today <stat> source=outreach_messages direction='outbound' (sent_at/created_at, channel). Today shown only as the Sent mailbox total (NudgeMailbox.tsx:222-227) metric=outbound message count today, split by channel
- Reply rate <stat> source=No page computes this today. It would derive from outreach_messages inbound vs outbound (nudge_threads) metric=inbound replies / outbound sends
- Audiences <stat> source=audience_plans (status counts), already computed client-side as chip counts and the Approvals badge metric=audience count + pending approval count
- Campaign performance (per run outcome tiles) <table> source=Nudge run stats API via NudgeRunStatsPanel (per campaign run), plus outreach_campaign_runs matched / accepted counts metric=Processing, Sent, Delivered, Failed, Opened, Soft bounced, Hard bounced, Spam, Dropped, Suppressed, Unsubscribed, No consent, Invalid per run
- Recent sends <feed> source=/api/omni/nudge/outbox (outreach_messages outbound, order created_at desc) metric=latest outbound messages: recipient, time, status, channel
- Templates in review <list> source=nudge_templates via /api/omni/templates (pendingTemplates filter) metric=templates with is_approved=false
- Applications stats <stat> source=/api/applications/stats (application_forms, application_submissions) metric=Application forms, Published, Submissions, Pending, Under Review, Approved, Rejected, Converted
- Social analytics KPIs <stat> source=social_posts via /api/social-posts/groups (published posts), computed client-side metric=Reach (sum impressions), Engagement Rate, Total Interactions (likes+comments+shares), Link Clicks
- Social platform comparison / sentiment / top posts <chart(bar) + list> source=social_posts metrics; useAnalyzeSentiment metric=per-platform metrics, sentiment split positive/neutral/negative (AI), top performing posts

## gap candidates
- Drag-and-drop content calendar (month/week, drag a post to reschedule) @ Social Hub › Schedule (components/social-hub/calendar/ContentCalendar.tsx, CalendarPostPill.tsx, CalendarDayCell.tsx; @dnd-kit): The ui-atoms calendar part is a date picker. It has no month/week event grid and no drag-to-reschedule.
- IVR flow builder canvas @ Templates (IVR/voice channel) -> IvrTemplateWorkspace / pages/ivr-flow-editor/IvrFlowEditor.tsx (@xyflow/react): A node/edge flow editor with undo/redo and auto-save. No ui-atoms part covers this.
- Rich-text / MJML email editor and sandboxed HTML preview @ Templates detail panel (components/omnichannel/EmailEditor.tsx uses tiptap + MJML compile), EmailTemplateEditorDialog / TemplatePreviewPane (iframe srcDoc), campaign MessageStepCard: A WYSIWYG rich-text editor and HTML email rendering in an iframe. textarea and composer cannot do this.
- Channel-specific template editors (SMS segment/encoding counter, WhatsApp, Push previews) @ NudgeDashboard TemplateDetailPanel (SmsEditor, WhatsAppEditor, PushEditor): Device-style previews and GSM/UCS-2 segment analysis go beyond plain form fields.
- Nested condition / query builder for audiences @ Audiences › New/Edit audience (components/outreach/PlanBuilder.tsx), campaign wizard 'Build a query' (CampaignAudienceEditor.tsx): Recursive AND/OR groups with field / operator / value rows and a live count. This is possibly composable from select + group + button, but it is a non-trivial recursive builder.
- SQL predicate editor @ AudienceSegmentsSettings predicate textarea (monospace SQL) + Test: Today it is a plain monospace textarea, which ui-atoms can render. A code editor with highlighting would be a gap if wanted.
- Charts in social analytics and run stats @ CCAnalyticsDashboard (Platform Comparison, Sentiment bars, Platform Metrics Comparison); Engage dashboard 'Campaign performance': No chart part in ui-atoms (only meter/progress).
- Application form builder (multi-step field designer with live preview, signature, payment and line-item fields, theme) @ ApplicationFormBuilder.tsx (Design / Distribute tabs; field types incl. signature :100; up/down reorder :886-910,1262-1264): A form-builder canvas with a live preview, a signature pad field preview and payment widgets. Reordering is done with buttons, not drag.
- CSV file upload for campaign audiences @ CampaignWizard › Audience › File feed (components/outreach/AudienceStep.tsx:127-147): Uses a file input with upload progress and match/reject counts. ui-atoms has no file drop zone part.
- Social post composer (multi-platform previews, AI compose/media, asset library, boost modals) @ SocialHub › Composer (components/social-hub/CCPostComposer.tsx, 1,873 lines), components/social/*: Per-platform post previews, media handling and character limits per platform are richer than the composer/textarea parts.
- Run pipeline stepper with timestamps @ OutreachCampaignDetail RunActivityView (:122-128,224-300): Probably covered by the stepper/timeline parts. Listed for confirmation only.

## open questions
- Sent list status rendering: NudgeMailbox.formatStatus maps numeric codes (NudgeMailbox.tsx:37-53,106-109), but the local outbox query returns outreach_messages.status as text (delivered / failed / opened / pending / sent; nudge-logs.service.ts:240-242). Non-failed rows likely render as 'status_sent' and similar. The status pills also send 'sent' / 'failed' / 'created'. Should the mock use the text enum?
- Sent rows always show 'Message' as the second line: nudgeName is not part of the local outbox payload. Should the mock show the campaign name, given that 14,650 of 25,059 persona outbound rows are campaign sends with no thread?
- Submissions has no status tab for 'pending_payment', which exists in QA (financial). Its status filter is not URL-backed, and the page has no pager beyond the first 50 rows. Should the mock add a Pending payment chip and pagination?
- /settings/audience-segments (AudienceSegmentsSettings, raw SQL saved segments) has no nav entry. Does it fold into Engage › Audiences as a second source type, or stay in Admin? The recon is silent.
- Nudge › IVR analytics (ivr-analytics-tab.tsx) and the 'Open Nudge Portal' / 'Register Nudge Account' actions are not assigned by the recon. Do they land in Engage (Templates or the dashboard) or in Support?
- Audience approvals: keep a separate Approvals tab/queue under Audiences, or a 'Pending approval' chip plus a review side panel? The same question applies to template approvals (Pending Approval dropdown) and social post approvals.
- Applications analytics placeholder (/applications/analytics) and 'Reply rate' on the recipe dashboard have no data source today. Should they be dropped or computed?
- Utilities persona (beacon) has 0 outreach_campaigns and 0 audience_plans in QA. Campaign/Audience mocks should take shapes from the financial / government_federal tenants.

## verifier: not ok claims
- [Nomenclature] Only the Applications pages call useIndustryLabel inside Engage, using the 4 keys applicationForms/submissions/applicationForm/submission, so no Engage label changes per industry :: ApplicationSubmissionDetail.tsx:149 also calls useIndustryLabel()('products') and uses it at :817-846 ('Invoice <term>' dialog, line labels). That resolves differently per persona: utilities 'Products' (industry-config.ts:103), financial 'Accounts & Services' (:271), government_federal 'Government Services' (:136). The four application keys do resolve to identical strings (:104-113, :272-281, :61-70).
- [Nomenclature] Engage nav group is navigation.ts:131-190 with no industry overrides :: File is apps/web/src/components/layout/navigation.ts. The ENGAGE group (key engage, :131-218) also holds Workflows (:191-202), TilliPay (:203-209) and Interactions (:210-217). The map does not mention them. No industry overrides confirmed.
- [Campaigns] Channel tabs order Email / SMS / WhatsApp / IVR :: Tabs are built from Object.keys(CHANNEL_LABELS), which is email, sms, ivr, whatsapp (OutreachCampaigns.tsx:49-54,74-75), so the rendered order is Email / SMS / IVR / WhatsApp.
- [Campaigns] Runs table columns # / Status / Matched / Accepted / Started / Launched :: Matched and Accepted are a single header 'Matched / Accepted' (OutreachCampaignDetail.tsx ~:379-383), so the table has 5 columns, not 6.
- [Campaigns] QA outreach_campaigns 52 rows overall :: SELECT count(*) FROM outreach_campaigns = 48. The map's own breakdown (29+11+6+1+1) also sums to 48.
- [Templates] QA active templates: utilities 70 (66 approved); financial 63 (56 approved); government_federal 67 (only 4 approved) :: Counting is_active and deleted_at null: utilities 70 with 65 approved, financial 63 with 56 approved, government_federal 67 with 3 approved. The channel splits match.
- [Templates] Top categories ... alert, network :: Across the 3 personas (active): application 30, null 25, service 25, agent_action 24, billing 24, alert 15, account 10, marketing 9, internal 9, onboarding 9, program 7, collections 7. 'network' is not in the top set, and null is the second-largest bucket.
- [Sent] /nudge?tab=sent renders NudgeMailbox mode=outbox at NudgeDashboard.tsx:303-305 :: The render is at NudgeDashboard.tsx:309-311 (mode="outbox" at :310). The content is otherwise correct.
- [Sent] Time = sent_at (sentOn) or scheduledOn :: The local payload has no scheduledOn, so a row with null sent_at shows an empty time. QA: 12,022 of 25,059 persona outbound rows have sent_at null.
- [Social Hub] QA social_posts financial ~59, gov ~108 across 9 platforms incl. snapchat :: Fresh query: financial 51 (7 platforms), government_federal 107 (8 platforms: facebook, instagram, linkedin, pinterest, snapchat, tiktok, twitter, youtube), utilities 11 facebook. No 'scheduled' status exists, so the Scheduled tab is empty.
- [Applications] QA forms gov 132 (published 115, archived 16); utilities 137; financial 42; 35 listed_public :: government_federal has 133 forms (published 115, archived 16, draft 2). The other counts match, and all 35 listed_public forms are in government_federal.
- [Applications] QA submissions government_federal 610; dates 2026-06-22..2026-10-28 :: government_federal has 605 (approved 196, converted 116, pending 108, under_review 93, rejected 92; sum 605). utilities 25 and financial 8 (pending_payment 2) match, and so does the date range.
- [Dashboard] Reply rate derivable from outreach_messages inbound vs outbound :: SELECT direction, count(*) FROM outreach_messages returns only outbound (29,180), with 0 inbound rows. Reply rate has no data source in QA.

## QA profile
notes: All 22 tables named in the map exist. audience_fields and audience_entities are not tenant-scoped; they are global catalogs. outreach_audience_files and audience_tenant_policy have no created_at column, so received_at and updated_at were used instead. These tables have 0 rows in the whole table: audience_tenant_policy, tenant_social_accounts, social_vendor_settings. outreach_saved_segments has 4 rows, all in a non-persona tenant.

Differences from the map:
- outreach_campaigns has 48 rows, not 52.
- government_federal social_posts cover 8 platforms, not 9.
- government_federal has 3 approved templates, not 4.
- outreach_messages is 100% outbound, so a 'Reply rate' cannot be derived from it.
- outreach_campaigns.last_run_at and last_run_id are null for every row, so 'Last run' would show 'Never run' even for completed campaigns.
- audience_plans are all approved at revision 1, so the non-approved status chips and the Approvals tab would be empty.
- outreach_campaign_groups have null cap values.
- The utilities persona has no campaigns, runs, audiences or audience files.

Enum counts use the persona with the most rows for each table. Per-persona variants are given where they differ meaningfully. All JSON keys and formats are shape-only; no personal values were included.

    enums: status=completed(9),draft(5),scheduled(2); channel=email(14),sms(2); mode=provisioned(16); audience_source=file(7),query(5),plan(4); schedule_kind=once_now(11),null(4),recurring(1); send_limit_type=none(14),max_number(2); cap_scope=null(15),campaign(1); recipient_rule=first_with_address(16); cap_key_type=recipient_id(16); readiness / last_run_outcome / trigger_event_type / on_stale_file=null(16)

## dashboard widgets
- Open deals <stat> source=GET /api/opportunities/stats -> open (today only on Opportunities.tsx stat cards) metric=count of opportunities with status=open (+ closing this month by expected_close_date)
- Pipeline <stat> source=GET /api/opportunities/stats -> totalValue (weighted value not computed today) metric=sum of open opportunity amount; weighted by probability
- Win rate / Won / Lost <stat> source=GET /api/opportunities/stats metric=won/(won+lost) %, won count + wonValue, lost count
- Lead funnel <stat> source=GET /api/leads/stats (useLeadStats) metric=Total, New, Contacted, Qualified, Converted counts
- Quotes awaiting <stat> source=quotes.status, quotes.valid_until; no stats endpoint today (would need useQuotes with status=sent) metric=count of quotes status=sent (+ expiring this week by valid_until)
- Orders this month <stat> source=orders list currencyTotals.count with dateFrom/dateTo filter metric=count of orders by order_date in month vs last month
- Pipeline by stage <chart(bar)> source=opportunities.stage_name/amount; no endpoint today metric=sum of opportunity amount grouped by stage_name
- Quotes expiring <list> source=quotes (status draft/sent, valid_until) metric=quotes not accepted ordered by valid_until
- Recent orders <table> source=GET /api/orders?direction=sales|purchase&sort=date&dir=desc metric=sales and purchase orders newest first

## QA profile
notes: All tables in the map exist: leads, opportunities, pipeline_stages, quotes, quote_line_items, orders, order_lines, plus order_documents (order to ar/ap link) and network_exchanges. ar and ap have no order_id column; the invoiced/billed amounts join through order_documents (document_type ar/ap). All of these tables have tenant_id. leads, opportunities, quotes and orders soft-delete with is_deleted/deleted_at, and the persona counts above are live rows. Totals including soft-deleted rows: utilities has 601 leads, 600 opportunities and 415 quotes, almost all soft-deleted. 'Most rows' persona per table: leads = government_federal (307 vs financial 296), opportunities, quotes, quote_line_items and orders = financial. Orders are thin per persona (financial 24, mostly purchase; utilities 16, mostly cancelled sales; gov 0), so status-by-direction uses the whole table (130 rows). Points for the mock: (1) Gov leads are 300 templated rows with only a title and no name, company or email, so the current UI shows '(No name)'. Financial leads are the better model: First Last + company + (NNN) NNN-NNNN phone. (2) Deal stages differ by tenant. Financial has 5 stages with 0-100 probability and colors (Discovery #3b82f6, Proposal #8b5cf6, Negotiation #f59e0b, Closed Won #22c55e, Closed Lost #ef4444). Gov has 7 stages with fractional probability, and no gov opportunity uses them (stage_name null). (3) Financial quote names, quote line-item product names and product codes reference a real card-issuer brand. Use neutral product names in the mock. (4) Order statuses in the data include in_progress, completed and scheduled (service and internal orders), which today's status filter does not list. received, partially_billed, closed_short and declined never occur. (5) Value scales differ a lot: deals median ~$700k (financial) or ~$9.4M (gov), quotes median ~$131k, orders median ~$80-120. (6) The DB date is 2026-11-10, and almost every quote's valid_until is in the past, so a 'Quotes expiring' panel built from QA would be empty. Shift the mock dates forward.

# MODULE finance  (verifier confidence: high)
nomenclature: The sidebar relabels item names through useNavigation (apps/web/src/hooks/useNavigation.ts:9-11, 52-56), which calls getLabel(item.key). Only the keys invoices, bills, payments and products exist in NomenclatureMap; subscriptions, ledger, rate-cards and cash-360 do not, so those keep their names. Values from lib/industry-config.ts: utilities (L94-133): invoices=Invoices, invoice=Invoice, bills=Bills, payments=Payments, products=Products, contacts/organizations=Contacts/Organizations. financial (L262-302): invoices=Invoices, bills=Bills, payments=Payments, products='Accounts & Services', contacts/organizations=Contacts/Organizations. government_federal (L136, which spreads GOVERNMENT_TERMS L50-90): invoices=Statements, invoice=Statement, bills=Bills, payments=Payments, products='Government Services' (an override of 'Permits & Licenses'), contacts=Constituents, organizations=Businesses. In the pages: the CustomerInvoices h1, subtitle, 'Create {invoice}' button, error title and pagination summary use getLabel (pages/CustomerInvoices.tsx:697, 702, 726, 1198, 1331). The Subscriptions first column header is getLabel('contacts') / getLabel('organizations') (pages/Subscriptions.tsx:239), and so is the customer picker placeholder (L186). The Products h1 is hard-coded 'Products & Services' (pages/Products.tsx:157); only the import wizard's entityLabel uses getLabel('products') (L50). VendorBills, Payments, Ledger, RecurringInvoices, ArOutbox, RateCards, Contracts and Cash360 use hard-coded labels. Tab labels are not translated (useNavigation.ts:61). Collision: for government_federal, 'Statements' as the invoices label clashes with Network › Statements (settlement statements) and with the proposed 'Partner settlements' filter.

countBadges: None on the Invoices nav today. The intent doc lists an 'Invoices › Overdue' dynamic count (03-intent.md:90), but useNavigation injects counts only for Bills and Exchanges (useNavigation.ts:57-60), Pagination summary 'Showing n of total {invoices}' (CustomerInvoices.tsx:1331), Recurring stats tiles: Active / Paused / This Week / Cancelled / Completed / Failed (RecurringScheduleStats.tsx:20-77), Statements accrual summary tiles per agreement and currency: Accrued / Invoiced / Settled (NetworkSettlementStatements.tsx:244-270)

pagination: Server offset pagination through the URL (useUrlPagination, default 25; CustomerInvoices.tsx:197) with Previous / 'Page x of y' / Next and 'Showing n of total' (L1328-1343). The recurring tab is not paginated. The Recurring page loads limit 1000 and filters on the client, no pagination (RecurringInvoices.tsx:22-25). The Outbox has a rows-per-page select of 10/25/50/100 (NudgeMailbox.tsx:428-436). Ready to invoice and Statements are not paginated (Ready to invoice shows a truncated notice).
empty: 'No invoices found' / 'Adjust search/filters or create a new invoice.' (CustomerInvoices.tsx:1208-1209); recurring tab: 'No recurring schedules found'. Recurring page: 'No recurring schedules found' / 'Try adjusting your filters' or 'Create recurring invoices from the invoice detail page' (RecurringInvoices.tsx:101-108). Outbox: 'No sent messages' or 'No messages match your filters' (NudgeMailbox.tsx:359). Ready to invoice: 'Nothing is waiting to be invoiced' (ReadyToInvoice.tsx:60). Statements: 'No settlement statements yet' / 'Settlement invoices will appear here after the next period close.' (NetworkSettlementStatements.tsx:275-278); disabled flag: 'Commercial settlement is not enabled' (L212-213).
recipeChanges: Merge Invoices (All plus status tabs), Recurring, Outbox and Ready to invoice into one Receivables list over ar. The ?status= sidebar tabs and in-page Tabs become filter chips (recon §5, last open point; recipe assumes chips). Recurring becomes a chip or segment, not a separate page. The Outbox mailbox becomes the delivery timeline on the invoice detail, with the mailbox as the platform thread component (rule 4, recon:32). Statements' AR side becomes a 'Partner settlements' filter on Receivables, with accrual lines and dispute actions in a side panel (rule 3, recon:90-91). Document exchanges become a delivery badge and timeline on the invoice detail (rule 3). Sidebar label Invoices -> Receivables (module table, recon:77).

recipeChanges: Bills becomes Payables. The status tabs become chips, with the pending, on-hold and rejected counts carried on the chips. Bills Inbox (a third mailbox over bill_inbox_messages, recon:32) folds in as an 'Inbox' chip or segment, or as the Conversations thread surface, instead of a separate route. The AP side of Statements becomes a 'Partner settlements' filter with accruals and disputes in a side panel (rule 3). The 'Bills to approve' panel goes on the Finance dashboard (modules.ts home panels). Purchase orders leave Finance for Sales › Orders.

recipeChanges: One Payments list in both directions, with 'In and out, newest first' as the dashboard panel (modules.ts). Today source tabs are forced and there is no consolidated view. The recipe direction is chips (direction, status, source) over one list. PaymentDetail stays as the detail. Cash 360 no longer gets its own sidebar entry; its figures feed the Finance dashboard.

recipeChanges: Catalogue stays inside Finance as one menu entry covering products, services, rate cards and plans (subscriptions), recon rule 7 (recon:105-106). Products/Services type tabs become chips. Products is a card grid today; the recipe can render it as a list or table. Subscriptions (customer plans) and rate cards become segments of Catalogue. Contracts is flagged and lives in Sales in today's nav, so its placement is open. MRR moves to the Finance dashboard (rule 1).

## dashboard widgets
- Receivable (open AR) <stat> source=Cash360 Receivables CashSection from useCash360 (pages/Cash360.tsx:191-192, CashSection L19-36); recipe stat 'Receivable … 14 overdue' (modules.ts:200) metric=Open receivables totalled by currency, with count
- Payable (open AP) <stat> source=Cash360 Payables CashSection (Cash360.tsx:188-189); the VendorBills pending count useStatusCount('pending_review') (VendorBills.tsx:85) metric=Open payables by currency + count; recipe hint 'n to approve' maps to the ap pending_review count
- Collected this month / Revenue This Month <stat> source=RevenueCard on the home Dashboard, /api/analytics/query/revenue-summary (components/analytics/RevenueCard.tsx:12-40; hooks/useInlineAnalytics.ts:61; Dashboard.tsx:1040) metric=revenueThisMonth in cents, changePercent vs last month, paymentSuccessRate
- Cash in 30/60/90 days <list> source=Cash360 ProjectionSection, a due-date projection (Cash360.tsx:93-126) metric=Projected inflow and outflow per currency for the next 30/60/90 days, plus overdue receivable/payable excluded
- MRR trend <chart(area sparkline)> source=MrrSparkline (recharts AreaChart), /api/analytics/query/mrr-trend (components/analytics/MrrSparkline.tsx:4-49; useInlineAnalytics.ts:46); moves from the home Dashboard per rule 1 metric=MRR over time
- MRR movement <chart(bar/area)> source=AnalyticsDashboard MRR section, mrr-trend query (components/analytics/dashboard-sections.tsx:109-124) metric=net_mrr_delta, new_mrr, churned_mrr per period
- Subscription stats <stat> source=Subscriptions page stats bar (pages/Subscriptions.tsx:124-163) metric=Active count, MRR total, Trials, Past Due

## gap candidates
- MRR sparkline and MRR movement chart @ components/analytics/MrrSparkline.tsx (recharts AreaChart), dashboard-sections.tsx:109-124: needs a chart library; no chart part in ui-atoms
- Aging bar chart, revenue and payments trend charts @ components/analytics/dashboard-sections.tsx:51-102: bar and area charts; no chart part
- Bill PDF scan drop zone @ components/billing/VendorBillUpload.tsx (react-dropzone, onDrop L17-26), used in VendorBills.tsx:293: file drop zone with drag-and-drop upload; no dropzone part
- Attachment / PDF preview @ BillsInbox.tsx:116-130 FilePreviewDialog: PDF/image viewer inside a dialog
- Invoice create line-item editor with recurrence builder and per-occurrence totals @ CustomerInvoices.tsx:736-1065, components/recurring/RecurrenceBuilder.tsx: a dynamic repeating-row form with product autocomplete, tax and discount per line; composable but heavy
- Product pricing tier editor @ Products.tsx:233-248 (PricingTierEditor): editable tier table with a live 'at qty N' preview
- Two-pane mailbox (AR Outbox, Bills Inbox) @ components/nudge/NudgeMailbox.tsx, BillsInbox.tsx: list plus detail-pane thread layout; composable from resizable, scroll-area and chat, but it is the platform thread component
- Hierarchical chart-of-accounts table @ ChartOfAccounts.tsx:283-296 (depth-indented rows): a tree inside a table (tree grid); tree and table are separate parts
- Expandable statement rows with nested accrual sub-table @ NetworkSettlementStatements.tsx:78-150: row expansion with a nested table; recipe moves it to a side panel (sheet)
- Select-all-matching bulk selection across pages @ CustomerInvoices.tsx:1166-1188: data-grid feature beyond a plain table

## open questions
- Contracts sits in the Sales group in today's nav (navigation.ts:269), but the task hints it belongs in Catalogue. Recon rule 7 lists 'products, services, rate cards, plans' and does not name contracts.
- Purchase orders, Work orders and Timesheets are Finance sidebar entries today (navigation.ts:321-326). The recon moves orders to Sales and timesheets to Operations; whether Finance keeps any of them is open (recon §5).
- Cash 360 is behind a flag (CASH_360_ENABLED). Should its due list and projection become the Finance dashboard, or a Payments segment?
- Should the Payments forced per-source tabs ('no consolidated view', Payments.tsx:125-127) survive, or does the redesign merge sources into one list with a source chip?
- government_federal relabels invoices as 'Statements', which collides with partner settlement statements. Should the Receivables filter be named something other than 'Statements' for that industry?
- Ready to invoice is an Orders tab today (OrderTabs). Does it become a Receivables chip or stay on Orders in Sales?
- Should Settings › Chart of Accounts merge into Ledger, or stay in Settings?

## verifier: not ok claims
- [Nomenclature] CustomerInvoices getLabel lines 697, 702, 726, 1198, 1331 :: Actual lines are h1 696, subtitle 703, button 726, error 1199 and summary 1331. Some uses are missing: DialogTitle 'New {invoice}' (732), the create_contact/create_organization links (761-762) and the notes placeholder (1009).
- [Nomenclature] Subscriptions customer picker placeholder uses getLabel contacts/organizations (L186) :: L186 uses the singular keys getLabel('contact') and getLabel('organization'). Only the header at L239 uses the plural keys.
- [Receivables] Source badge shows provider values such as oracle_ccnb, customer_subscription or none :: SourceBadge.tsx:11-18 always renders. It shows the xdex integration label when the source is known, otherwise 'ERP' (source==='erp'), otherwise 'Beacon'. Null and unregistered sources render as 'Beacon', not as nothing.
- [Receivables] Statements row actions: expand, Mark paid (obligor), Dispute per accrual :: The map misses the PaymentNoticeActions on every row (L94; SettlementPaymentActions.tsx). The payee side has Confirm received / Not received / Already recorded; the payer side has Report again. The dispute button label is 'Open dispute' (L144).
- [Payables] Status-tab counts on Pending, On hold and Rejected :: useStatusCount(status, approvalOn, …) returns null unless apApprovalEnabled() (VendorBills.tsx:55-58,84-87). With the approval flag off there are no counts, including on Pending.
- [Payables] ap status enum list in the Status column :: ap_status also includes 'disputed' (enum_range ap_status)
- [Payments] Source tabs are the fixed list oracle_ccnb, sap_s4hana, quickbooks, freshbooks, xero, hubspot, manual, terminal, tillipay :: The tabs are built at runtime: distinct pay.provider values except tillipay, plus 'terminal' when payment_transactions exist, plus 'tillipay' always (Payments.tsx:125-130; api card-payments.service.ts getPaymentSources ~1101-1121). SOURCE_LABELS (L82-93) is only a label map. In QA the tabs are 'Internal' + TilliPay, and financial also gets Terminal.
- [Payments] Status badge values include completed :: statusConfig (L28-40) has no 'completed', so financial's 10,330 completed rows render as an unstyled raw 'completed'. The status filter offers no completed, processing or reversed, though pay_status has them.
- [Payments] Pagination: PAGE_SIZE 20, server-side per source :: Local sources page on the server. The TilliPay tab is fetched unfiltered from /api/payments/tillipay, then filtered and sliced on the client (Payments.tsx:147-170).
- [Catalogue] Products list reflects QA counts (utilities 1145) :: /api/products applies no default status filter (products.routes.ts:122-141), and the card shows no status. Utilities' 1126 archived products (only 19 active) are listed among the rest and cannot be told apart.
- [Dashboard] Widget list covers Cash360 :: The list leaves out the 'Payments made' and 'Payments received' CashSections (Cash360.tsx:193-196) and PaidViewSection (L129-165,199). It also skips the positionShown gate, which hides Receivables and the projection.

## QA profile
notes: All Finance tables in the map exist except 'uomConfigs' is named "uomConfigs" (camelCase, 0 rows) and products uses camelCase columns. Line items: invoice_line_items exists but has 0 rows for the personas; oracle-sourced ar/ap embed metadata.lineItems (1-7, median 2). Not tenant_id-scoped: network_commercial_accruals/disputes (beneficiary/obligor tenant ids), network_commercial_settlement_facts (owner_tenant_id). Empty tables: ap_approvals, ap_disputes, bill_inbox_rejections, contracts, contract_versions, network_commercial_disputes, uomConfigs. Utilities ar is 128 rows (not 87k as the task stated). Enum definitions: ar_status draft/sent/viewed/partially_paid/paid/overdue/void/written_off/open; ar_type invoice/credit_note/payment/refund; ap_status draft/pending_review/approved/scheduled/paid/partially_paid/overdue/void/disputed/rejected/on_hold; ap_type bill/credit_note/debit_note; pay_method card/ach/wire/check/cash/stablecoin/virtual_card/other; pay_status pending/processing/approved/settled/completed/failed/refunded/reversed/cancelled; ap_dispute_status raised/under_review/resolved/withdrawn; account_type customer/vendor/employee/household/internal/unclassified. All money is USD. Ready-to-invoice would be empty in QA (orders.billing_state all 'none', time_entries 2 non-billable). Utilities chart_of_accounts are inactive E2E test rows; government_federal holds the clean 10-account system CoA.

# MODULE operations  (verifier confidence: high)
nomenclature: Only Outages is renamed per industry, and only on the Dashboard map title (terminology.outageMap); the sidebar label 'Outages' (navigation.ts:391) and page title 'Outage Management' (OutageManagement.tsx:121) are hardcoded. lib/industry-config.ts: utilities outage/outage_map/outage_management = 'Outage' / 'Outage Map' / 'Outage Management' (lines 116-118); financial = 'System Outage' / 'System Map' / 'System Status' (lines 284-286); government_federal uses GOVERNMENT_TERMS = 'Disaster/Weather' / 'Disaster Map' / 'Emergency Management' (lines 73-75, 136). The Dashboard map renders only for utilities/telecom (Dashboard.tsx:333). Calendar, Services, Staff, Work orders and Timesheets have no per-industry relabels. The outages page requires industry module outageManagement (OutageManagement.tsx:95; hooks/useSettings.ts:23).

## gap candidates
- Staff resource calendar (day/week, one column per staff member, click-empty-slot to book, now-scroll, hidden-events note) @ StaffCalendar.tsx:160-187 (CalendarGrid from @tillibeacon/ui): ui-atoms 'calendar' is a date picker; a resource time grid with events and slot selection is not a simple composition
- Reschedule slot picker (two-week DayStrip with per-day counts + TimeSlotGrid with quick picks and next opening) @ AppointmentDetail.tsx:282-285: custom availability grid; could be approximated with toggle-group/segmented-control but slot density and counts are bespoke
- Outage map with Google Maps markers, GeoJSON boundaries and info windows @ Dashboard.tsx:430-700, 1456-1510: map rendering is not in ui-atoms
- Weekly hours editor (per-weekday multiple time ranges, copy to all days) @ SchedulingStaff.tsx:300-350: repeatable time-range rows per weekday; composable from fields but needs a time input which ui-atoms lacks
- Order list column chooser + per-currency totals footer + bulk action bar with per-row skip reasons @ KindOrders.tsx:153-165, 229; OrderListColumns.tsx:45-80; OrderBulkActions.tsx: data-grid features (column visibility persistence, footer aggregates, selection bulk runner) beyond plain table

## open questions
- Service orders and Ready to invoice: Operations > Work orders (as a kind tab) or Sales > Orders (recon §4 Sales menu 'Orders (direction as a tab)')? Ready to invoice is a billing queue that may belong in Finance > Receivables.
- Timesheets placement: Operations (recommended) vs Finance (recon §5 line 112).
- Whether order status/facet tabs become filter chips (recon §5 last bullet; recipe assumes chips).
- Outage map today renders only for utilities/telecom; should the Operations dashboard hide it for financial and government_federal, or show the renamed 'System Map' / 'Disaster Map'?
- Outages are gated by tickets.view today; Operations may need its own permission.
- QA persona data is thin: government_federal has no appointments, staff, services, orders, time or outages; only one submitted time entry exists in all QA, so the Timesheets mock needs synthetic rows.

## QA profile
notes: All tables in the map exist. Name mapping: 'staff availability (rules, exceptions)' = availability_rules + availability_exceptions; offering staff = service_offering_staff; materials = order_materials; order lines = order_lines. There are also related tables the map does not list: staff_bookings, appointment_attendees, appointment_links, appointment_reminders, service_orders, utility_service_orders, outage_affected_accounts (66 rows). These were not profiled. service_offering_staff has tenant_id but no org_id or created_at. Every other profiled table is tenant-scoped.

Persona coverage is thin. government_federal has 0 rows in every Operations table. Utilities has only cancelled appointments, inactive services, non-bookable staff, cancelled service orders, no work orders and no time entries. Financial has 3 work orders (all with total 0), 2 approved time entries and 1 outage. Because of this, each table's shapeNotes also gives whole-table (ALL) distributions, which are the better base for realistic mocks.

Notes per item:
- Calendar: 77 appointments in total, created 2026-10-25..2026-11-03, starts 2026-10-25..2026-11-06. 51 are not cancelled, across 22 distinct hosts. Duration median is 60 min. no_show_party is null everywhere and late_cancel is false everywhere, so the 'Late cancel' badge and 'Did not attend' need synthetic rows.
- Timesheets: there is exactly 1 submitted time_entry in all of QA. minutes is 0..3 (test artefacts), notes is null everywhere, and every row has source=appointment. The mock should synthesise realistic 15..480 min entries.
- Outages: 52 of 53 use number format BCN-XXXXXXXX-XX (base36) and 1 uses CM-OUT-####-####. customers_affected is 0 for every utilities row (max 414 in all QA). customers_restored and notifications_sent are 0 everywhere. boundary is null for utilities. area_criteria for utilities holds cities only, 1 city per outage. Utilities has 1 active (not closed/restored) outage and 10 exist in all QA.
- Work orders: 17 of 18 rows have cost_center, using codes like DEMO-CC, MAINT-NORTH, FLEET, SAFETY. Totals are mostly 0, max 5600 USD. billing_state is always 'none'.
- Service orders: all are USD. billing_state is none or partially_invoiced only, with no 'invoiced'. billing_mode is per_appointment or on_completion.
- QA holds only USD order data. Staff rate currencies vary: USD, EUR, INR, GBP, JPY, MXN and one invalid 'ZZZ'.
- Outage enum labels in pg_enum:
  - status: reported, confirmed, crew_assigned, crew_en_route, crew_on_site, restored, closed
  - type: planned, unplanned, emergency, partial
  - cause: weather, equipment, vegetation, vehicle, animal, overload, maintenance, unknown

# MODULE support  (verifier confidence: high)
nomenclature: Only the Tickets noun is renamed. The sidebar item key 'tickets' (navigation.ts:390) goes through useNavigation getLabel(toNomenclatureKey(item.key), item.name) (hooks/useNavigation.ts:49-52). lib/industry-config.ts gives: utilities tickets='Tickets' (industry-config.ts:94-100); financial tickets='Service Requests' (industry-config.ts:262-268); government_federal = {...GOVERNMENT_TERMS} where tickets='Service Requests' (industry-config.ts:50-57, 136). Other industries, for reference: 'Support Cases' (:145), 'Appointments' (:186), 'Support Tickets' (:227), 'Claims' (:309), 'Tickets' (:350). The Tickets page itself does NOT relabel its h1 ('Tickets', Tickets.tsx:297), its 'Create Ticket' button or its empty state. It only uses getLabel for the Contact and Organization pickers in the Create dialog (Tickets.tsx:718-729; government gives 'Constituent'/'Businesses', industry-config.ts:51-52,64). CsrInbox, NudgeMailbox, Interactions, CsrConsole and the Dashboard contact-center widgets have no industry relabeling. The section header 'SUPPORT' (navigation.ts:386) is not translated (useNavigation.ts:27-28 has that code commented out). QA shape per persona: utilities (beacon) has 8 tickets (all new/medium, #7..#2210), 84 nudge_escalations (mostly resolved, priority low/medium/high/critical, category billing/Safety/null) and 550 interactions (voice/sms/email/chat, all completed). financial ([financial persona tenant]) has 489 tickets: waiting_internal 213, closed 89, resolved 58, new 26, confirmed 24, in_progress 23, triaged 20, waiting_customer 16, assigned 15, reopened 5. Its categories are card_services/technical/account_maintenance/merchant_services/disputes, 487/489 have an SLA due date and 376 are assigned. It has 851 interactions: voice/sms/email/chat in and out, statuses completed and missed, durations 3s-1799s. government_federal ([government_federal persona tenant]) has 1213 tickets spread evenly over 7 statuses and 4 priorities, with about 20 agency categories (FOIA Request, Security Clearance, Compliance Audit and so on). None of its tickets has an SLA due date or an assignee, and it has 1 interaction. No persona tenant has inbound outreach_messages (QA has 0 inbound rows at all), so Nudge Inbox renders empty everywhere. nudge_threads are all status=open, priority=normal, unread_count=0, and none has a ticket_id.

recipeChanges: Rule 4: Support > Inbox becomes the single triage mailbox in the NudgeMailbox layout (list pane plus thread pane). It merges inbound Nudge threads (outreach_messages + nudge_threads) and CSR escalations (nudge_escalations), and the interactions call log becomes a 'Calls' channel inside it instead of the separate /interactions page. Tickets are the case object a thread attaches to (nudge_threads.ticket_id). /tickets/inbox and /nudge?tab=inbox stop being separate pages; Nudge > Calls (scaffold) and the dead communication-hub inbox-tab/sent-tab are removed; Sent moves to Engage. Status tabs and channel pills become filter chips (recon section 5 says the recipe assumes chips). The CSR capacity strip, agent load bars and the JAREIS suggested reply carry over into the thread pane. Escalation rows should show the linked ticket (nudge_escalations.ticket_id) and thread. Recipe stats: 'Inbox unread', '9 escalated by AI' (modules.ts:269).

## Tickets
pages: /tickets -> pages/Tickets.tsx (App.tsx:262; nav Support > Tickets navigation.ts:390, permission tickets.view); /tickets/:id -> pages/TicketDetail.tsx (App.tsx:263) with components/tickets/TicketActivityTabs.tsx; /csr-console -> pages/CsrConsole.tsx (App.tsx:266, behind the VITE_CSR_CONSOLE_ENABLED flag): its nav links to Tickets/CSR inbox; the customer lookup belongs with Agent desk
tables: tickets, ticket_comments, ticket_attachments, ticket_sla_policies, nudge_threads (email tab via ticket_id), users, contacts, organizations
columns: # <text '#<n>' (muted); detail page shows 'TKT-<n>' (TicketDetail.tsx:196)> (tickets.ticket_number) [none] | Subject <link (/tickets/:id) + muted category subline> (tickets.subject, with tickets.category as a second line) [none] | Customer <link (/contacts/:id, /organizations/:id)> (tickets.contact_id -> contacts first/last name (link), with tickets.organization_id -> organizations.name as a second line; falls back to the org name, else em dash) [column header stays 'Customer'] | Status <badge(status) pill with icon, title = description> (tickets.status mapped by TICKET_STATUSES (new, triaged, assigned, in_progress, waiting_customer, waiting_internal shown as 'Escalated', resolved, confirmed, closed, reopened)) [none] | Priority <badge(status) StatusBadge preset ticketPriority> (tickets.priority (low/medium/high/urgent)) [none] | SLA <badge: 'SLA met' / 'SLA overdue by Xm|h|d' / 'SLA due in X' with a clock icon; title = full datetime; nothing when null> (tickets.sla_due_at, resolved when status is in resolved/confirmed/closed) [none] | Assigned To <text; orange 'Unassigned' when null> (tickets.assigned_to -> users name (assignedToName)) [none] | Actions <icon buttons (view, delete)> (-) [none]
filters: search/Search tickets... (URL ?q=, 300ms debounce): free text ; select/Filter by status (URL ?status=): All Statuses, New, Triaged, Assigned, In Progress, Waiting Customer, Escalated (waiting_internal), Resolved, Confirmed, Closed, Reopened ; toggle/My tickets (URL ?view=mine -> assignedTo=current user): mine ; toggle/Platform Support (provider users only, URL ?cat=ps -> category=platform_support): ps ; status-tab/Status group buttons (URL ?group= plus comma-joined ?status=): All, Open (new,triaged,assigned,in_progress,waiting_customer,waiting_internal,reopened), New, In Progress, Waiting (waiting_customer,waiting_internal), Resolved (resolved,confirmed), Closed
rowActions: View Ticket (Eye icon, links to /tickets/:id), Tickets.tsx:664-668, Delete Ticket (Trash icon, gated on tickets.delete, opens ConfirmDialog), Tickets.tsx:669-679, Subject link -> /tickets/:id; Customer links -> contact/org detail, Detail page: change Status (SimpleSelect plus TicketStatusStepper), change Priority, Assign to (AgentSelect, tickets.assign), Escalate to agent with a reason (tickets.update), activity tabs All/Comments/Email/Files/Linked/History/Service Requests, 360 Snapshot side card (contact, org, assigned, category, related/open tickets), Jareis insight cards and 'Generate Suggestions'
pageActions: Create Ticket (gated on tickets.create). Dialog fields: subject, description, priority, category, contact and organization pickers, assignee, SLA due, staged linked records and file attachments (Tickets.tsx:56-63, 317-322, 705-770), CSR Inbox link button (csr.queue.view), Tickets.tsx:309-316, Process SLA Breaches (approval.decide) in the SLA monitoring card, Tickets.tsx:409-418, No import, export or bulk actions on the list
countBadges: KPI tiles: Total, Open, In Progress, Waiting (customer + internal), Resolved (resolved + confirmed), Closed from useTicketStats (Tickets.tsx:327-355), Status Breakdown: per-status counts; Priority Mix: low/medium/high/urgent counts (Tickets.tsx:358-394), SLA: Open Tickets, Past SLA, Escalated plus 'Top overdue tickets' (top 5 with 'Nm overdue') (Tickets.tsx:420-453), No sidebar badge (hooks/useNavigation.ts:57-60)
sorting: None in the UI; server order.
pagination: Shared @tillibeacon/ui Pagination with URL-synced page and page size (20/50/100, default 50; the API caps at 100), pageCount from data.total (Tickets.tsx:68, 127, 687-697).
empty: 'No tickets found' / 'Try adjusting your search or filter criteria.' when searching or filtering; otherwise 'No tickets yet' / 'Create your first ticket to start tracking customer issues.' plus a Create Ticket button (Tickets.tsx:527-547). Error: 'Failed to load tickets. Please try again.' SLA card: 'No SLA breaches detected.'
recipeChanges: Tickets stays the Support case list, with the menu label relabeled per industry (Service Requests for financial and government_federal). Status group buttons and the status select collapse into filter chips (recon section 5), with 'My tickets' as a chip; the 'My tickets' card moves to Enterprise > Inbox (rule 1). The KPI tiles, status/priority breakdown and SLA breach monitor move to the Support dashboard ('Open tickets', 'SLA at risk', 'SLA watch' panel in modules.ts:270-279). The detail page gains a linked-thread / conversation side panel because a ticket is the case object threads attach to (rule 4; nudge_threads.ticket_id). Tickets generated by outages stay here while Outages moves to Operations (rule 6). The CSR Inbox header button goes away because Inbox is a sibling menu item.

## Agent desk
