recipeChanges: Rule 5: Agent desk replaces Nudge > Agent. The mock agent panel is deleted, and the real pieces move off the home Dashboard into it: the Connect softphone (CcpPanel phone dock), live caller context / screen pop, my presence selector, team roster (agent_status + open tickets), Live Agent Status, call KPIs and the hourly volume chart (rule 1: 'contact center pulse and softphone to Support > Agent desk'), and 'my queue' widgets (CSR My Queue capacity). The CsrConsole customer lookup, summary and timeline are a natural fit for the caller-context pane. /contact-center and /nudge?tab=agent are retired. The recipe's 'Agents online' stat and 'Agent status' panel (modules.ts:273, 280-282) reflect this.

## dashboard widgets
- Contact Center call KPIs (Total, Answered, Missed, Wait, Handle, SLA %, Abandon %) <stat> source=GET /api/agent-stats/dashboard (useAgentDashboard) over interactions metric=totalCalls, answeredCalls, missedCalls, avgWaitTime, avgHandleTime, serviceLevelPercent (green >= 80), abandonRate (green <= 5) for the selected preset range
- Hourly Call Volume <chart(bar)> source=agentData.hourly from /api/agent-stats/dashboard (interactions) metric=calls per hour 00-23 (CSS bar heights)
- Live Agent Status <list> source=agentData.liveAgents (agent_status) metric=name, email, presence badge per online agent
- Open Tickets KPI <stat> source=dashboard summary over tickets metric=summary.openTickets (links to /tickets)
- Interactions today (Mine/Other) <stat> source=dashboard summary over interactions metric=interactionsToday.mine / interactionsToday.others
- Recent Tickets <list> source=summary.recentTickets (tickets) metric=#ticketNumber, subject, status pill, priority pill (links to ticket)
- Ticket status KPIs (Total, Open, In Progress, Waiting, Resolved, Closed) <stat> source=useTicketStats (tickets) metric=counts by status group
- Status Breakdown / Priority Mix <list> source=useTicketStats metric=count per status (10) and per priority (4)
- SLA Breach Monitoring (SLA watch) <table> source=useTicketSlaStats, useTicketSlaBreaches(8) over tickets + ticket_sla_policies metric=Open Tickets, Past SLA, Escalated; top 5 overdue tickets with minutes overdue; Process SLA Breaches action
- CSR capacity / triage queue <stat> source=useCsrWorkload over nudge_escalations metric=escalation counts by status (pending/assigned/in_progress/resolved/closed), SLA breaches, active agents, per-agent load % bars
- Inbox unread <stat> source=GET /api/omni/stats (useInboxStats) metric=totalUnread
- Interactions by channel <chart(bar)> source=analytics query 'interaction-volume' (interactions) metric=event_count per channel

## gap candidates
- Amazon Connect softphone (CcpPanel iframe) with screen pop, phone dock and call controls @ pages/Dashboard.tsx:1556-1615; components/ccp/CcpPanel; agent-panel-tab.tsx dialpad: An embedded third-party CCP iframe plus dialpad and call timer; no softphone or dialpad part in ui-atoms
- Hourly Call Volume bar chart and Interactions-by-channel chart @ pages/Dashboard.tsx:1390-1418; components/analytics/dashboard-sections.tsx:154-166: Charts; ui-atoms has no chart part (meter/progress only)
- Drag an escalation row onto an agent load bar to assign @ pages/CsrInbox.tsx:159-173, 284-321, 433-435: HTML5 drag-and-drop between a table and drop targets; no dnd part
- Two-pane mailbox (list + thread/detail + composer) as the platform thread component @ components/nudge/NudgeMailbox.tsx; CsrInbox detail panel; recon rule 4: Probably composable from resizable + scroll-area + chat + composer, but threading, channel icons and AI reply approval need a dedicated pattern
- Rich multi-channel composer (NewMessagePanel / omnichannel Composer with templates, email HTML) @ components/nudge/NudgeMailbox.tsx:471-520: Email composition may need a rich-text/HTML editor beyond the composer/textarea parts
- Ticket attachments staged in the create dialog (StagedFilePicker) and the Files tab @ pages/Tickets.tsx:40, 764; components/tickets/TicketActivityTabs.tsx:287: File drop zone / upload; no file-drop part
- Ticket status stepper with 10 statuses including branches (waiting/escalated/reopened) @ pages/TicketDetail.tsx TicketStatusStepper: stepper exists, but non-linear states may not fit a linear stepper
- CallScriptEditor @ pages/CsrConsole.tsx:181: Script editing likely needs a rich-text editor

## open questions
- Recon rule 1 sends 'my tickets' and the inbox to Enterprise > Inbox. Do Dashboard 'Recent Tickets' and the 'Open Tickets' KPI land on the Support dashboard or in Enterprise?
- Do the Support dashboard and Agent desk split the contact-center widgets (KPIs and hourly chart on the dashboard; softphone, presence and roster on the desk), or do all of them go to Agent desk as rule 1 literally says?
- QA has zero inbound outreach_messages and no nudge_threads with ticket_id. The merged Inbox mock needs synthetic inbound threads; escalations in QA have no thread_id and almost no ticket_id.
- Utilities interactions in QA have started_at at the 2000-01-01 epoch placeholder, so date presets would hide them all.
- Should the CsrConsole (flagged off, REQ-350-363 deferred) be folded into Agent desk caller context, or stay out of scope?
- Tickets list has no ?status= sidebar tabs in navigation.ts. The in-page ?group= buttons are the candidates for chips. Should Platform Support (provider only) remain a chip?

## verifier: not ok claims
- [nomenclature] Government Create dialog picker labels are 'Constituent'/'Businesses' :: The label uses the singular getLabel('organization') = 'Business' (industry-config.ts:65). 'Businesses' appears only in the search placeholder and empty text (getLabel('organizations')).
- [nomenclature] Utilities has 84 nudge_escalations :: SQL gives 81 for the utilities tenant. 84 is the whole-table total (3 rows belong to other tenants).
- [nomenclature] Utilities escalation categories are billing/Safety/null :: SQL: null 45, billing 31, Safety 4, technical 1
- [Inbox] Nudge Inbox data comes from GET /api/nudge inbox over outreach_messages inbound joined to nudge_threads :: The endpoint is GET /api/omni/nudge/inbox (hooks/useNudge.ts:59; api routes/omnichannel.routes.ts:774). Inbound filter at nudge-logs.service.ts:106 and thread left join at :178,209 are correct.
- [Inbox] Sorting: escalations and inbox newest first, interactions by started_at :: Escalations are ordered by priority rank (critical first), then created_at ascending (oldest first): escalation.service.ts:275 with PRIORITY_RANK at :282. Inbox is desc(createdAt) (nudge-logs.service.ts:226). Interactions are desc(started_at), desc(created_at) (interactions.service.ts:212).
- [Tickets] Persona ticket counts are what the list shows :: For non-provider tenants GET /api/tickets and /api/tickets/stats exclude category platform_support (tickets.routes.ts:536-544, 346-349). All three personas have billing_source invoice_contract, so they are non-provider: utilities shows 6 of 8 and financial 488 of 489.
- [Agent desk] Dashboard Live Agent Status lists online agents from agent_status :: getLiveAgentStatus (agent-stats.service.ts:258-276) selects active agent-role users LEFT JOIN agent_status with COALESCE(status,'offline'), excluding the viewer. Offline agents are listed too.
- [dashboard] Interactions by channel widget belongs on the Support dashboard per recon redistribution :: It is an Analytics page chart (components/analytics/dashboard-sections.tsx:154-166), not on the home Dashboard. Recon rule 1 does not redistribute it, and recon line 73 keeps Analytics in Enterprise.
- [profile] Utilities nudge_escalations dateRange 2026-09-30..2026-10-19 :: Utilities rows span 2026-10-09..2026-10-19 (audit log the same). 2026-09-30 is the minimum across all of QA.

## QA profile
notes: All tables in the map exist. ticket_attachments and ticket_sla_policies are EMPTY in all of QA (0 rows), so the Files tab, attachment mock and SLA policy matching have no QA reference. tenant_csr_settings has 1 row in all of QA (utilities): max_escalations_per_agent 10, default_priority medium, auto_assign false, sla_thresholds {}, escalation_categories [], auto_send_threshold 90, escalation_threshold 60, countdown_seconds 3, rebalance_threshold 90. Corrections to the map: utilities has 81 nudge_escalations (84 is the whole-table total); financial and government have 0 escalations. outreach_messages is 100% outbound across all of QA (29180 rows), so Nudge Inbox is empty everywhere. nudge_threads: 0 rows in all of QA have ticket_id; all are status open, priority normal, unread_count 0, unassigned; last_message_direction is outbound. Escalations: 0/81 have thread_id, 1/81 has ticket_id, only 3 have the metadata aiConfidence key (value 72), suggestedReply and suggestedMessageId, and 66/81 have resolution_type test_cleanup. The mock should invent inbound threads, pending/assigned/in_progress escalations, AI confidence (50-99%) and ticket links. Interactions: utilities started_at is all at the 2000-01-01 placeholder with 1s duration. Financial started_at runs 2026-04-12..2026-09-18 (none in the last 30 days, so every date preset is empty), evenly spread over hours 09-16. from/to numbers use the format '(999) 999-9999' on one side and NULL on the other (inbound has from set, outbound has to set). There are 2 distinct agents, hold/wrap-up are always 0, and disposition, wrap_up_code and recording_url are always null. Tickets: financial sla_due_at is created_at +24h/72h/120h (by priority), and every due date falls 2026-03..07, so 316 of the open tickets read as overdue and only 1 distinct assignee exists. Government tickets have no assignee, SLA, first response, escalation or resolution_type. agent_status has 5 rows in QA (utilities 1 available, financial 1 available + 1 offline, government 1 available), with no manual_status/status_source. users.role is mostly null (government 2 tenant_admin). ticket_comments: utilities 4, financial 5, government 0, all is_internal=false.

Financial sla_due_at falls in 2026-03-14..2026-07-16, so open tickets are all overdue against now. Financial contacts and orgs are mostly mutually exclusive: contacts are 'First Last', orgs are business names. Government tickets almost all link both a contact and an org (org names are mostly agency-style names). Government tickets have no assignee, SLA, first_response, escalated_to or resolution_type. 0 tickets are linked from nudge_threads. (p50 0 = not computed.)
